# Manual expired-item disposition checkpoint

**PROPOSALS ONLY — no manual disposition has been applied.** Handoff 5673501235 carries DA ruling 5673485508 / repair commit cecb22588ad9d5c8663eff0429f22a2b09bbcb82. Exact base is c26f02270ed16749170c780c4d9cadb856f51a2b. This proposal set covers every remaining expired identity after the ruled safe reap: 279 items. The TSV is the machine-readable full table; all columns are reproduced below.

`DELETE` on a parked identity means that exact expired function and pen row, never its whole shared file. `ELEVATE` on a mixed test-file lease promotes only the container/support ownership, never its non-expired test identities or lease dates. Current-track proof promotions are finite `until-closeout:*` dispositions, not new seven-day leases or permanent test exemptions. Production-file promotions preserve source bytes and E8 identities; the named cost debts remain with the current research inventory. Historical code proposed for `docs/archive/` becomes non-runnable reference evidence; it is not a validated current instrument. Only the exact retained A1 harness/shader/notes are proposed as the old comparison arm.

Two `generator_cli` rows propose real code elevation to owning-crate binaries with named script caller updates; they are active generator implementations, not removable test assertions. This protected companion work is proposed explicitly for approval and has not been performed.

Consumer entries identify observed committed call/import/harness references, not new runtime execution. All owner paths and named referee functions were checked to exist; no exact proposed DELETE referee is used as another row's retained owner. An old parked note or `consumer=PERFORMANCE-TRACK` is not used as an elevation basis. The cited workplan/exposure rows distinguish committed future obligations from execution already done. Orchestration must approve this table before source/ledger promotion, deletion or rehome.

Proposed totals: **DELETE 88; ELEVATE 191**. All originating dates below are preserved audit facts.
